<script>
  // Configurar workers antes de cargar Monaco. Devolvemos un worker vacío
  // para silenciar los 404 y la creación de workers que no usamos (TS, etc.).
  self.MonacoEnvironment = {
    getWorker: function () {
      const blob = new Blob(['self.onmessage=()=>{};'], { type: 'application/javascript' });
      return new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(blob));
    }
  };

// Exponer globalmente para uso desde el script principal
window.buildErpProject = buildErpProject;

<script type="module">
  import { compile as svelteCompile } from 'https://esm.sh/svelte@5/compiler';

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // FileSystem — CRUD + persistencia + observer
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const fs = {
    _files: new Map(),
    _subs: new Set(),
    _key: 'sivo-app-studio:project:v1',
    _legacyKey: 'repl-svelte:project:v3',

    /** Carga desde localStorage. Llamar una vez al inicio. */
    load() {
      try {
        let raw = localStorage.getItem(this._key);
        // Migración automática desde la key vieja (repl-svelte) si la nueva no existe.
        if (!raw && this._legacyKey) {
          raw = localStorage.getItem(this._legacyKey);
          if (raw) {
            try { localStorage.setItem(this._key, raw); } catch {}
            try { localStorage.removeItem(this._legacyKey); } catch {}
          }
        }
        if (!raw) return;
        const data = JSON.parse(raw);
        for (const path in data) {
          this._files.set(path, { content: data[path], mtime: 0 });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('localStorage corrupto, ignorando:', e);
        localStorage.removeItem(this._key);
      }
    },

    /** Devuelve true si existe el archivo. */
    exists(path) { return this._files.has(path); },

    /** Devuelve el contenido o lanza si no existe. */
    read(path) {
      if (!this._files.has(path)) throw new Error('No existe: ' + path);
      return this._files.get(path).content;
    },

    /** Escribe. Dispara observers si cambió el contenido. */
    write(path, content) {
      const wasNew = !this._files.has(path);
      const prev = wasNew ? null : this._files.get(path).content;
      this._files.set(path, { content, mtime: Date.now() });
      this._save();
      if (wasNew || prev !== content) {
        this._notify({ type: 'write', path, prev, next: content });
      }
    },

    rename(from, to) {
      if (this._files.has(to)) throw new Error('Ya existe: ' + to);
      if (!this._files.has(from)) throw new Error('No existe: ' + from);
      const entry = this._files.get(from);
      this._files.delete(from);
      this._files.set(to, entry);
      this._save();
      this._notify({ type: 'rename', from, to, content: entry.content });
    },

    delete(path) {
      if (!this._files.has(path)) return;
      const entry = this._files.get(path);
      this._files.delete(path);
      this._save();
      this._notify({ type: 'delete', path, content: entry.content });
    },

    /** Lista los paths ordenados. */
    list() { return [...this._files.keys()].sort(); },

    /** Suscribe a cambios. Devuelve función para desuscribir. */
    subscribe(cb) {
      this._subs.add(cb);
      return () => this._subs.delete(cb);
    },

    _save() {
      try {
        const data = {};
        for (const [path, entry] of this._files) data[path] = entry.content;
        localStorage.setItem(this._key, JSON.stringify(data));
      } catch (e) {
        if (e.name === 'QuotaExceededError') {
          throw new Error('Proyecto demasiado grande para guardar');
        }
        throw e;
      }
    },

    _notify(event) {
      for (const cb of this._subs) {
        try { cb(event); } catch (e) { console.error('fs subscriber error:', e); }
      }
    },
  };

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Resolver — paths + extensión opcional
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const resolver = {
    /** Une base + relative normalizando (./ ../ /). */
    _join(base, relative) {
      if (relative.startsWith('./')) relative = relative.slice(2);
      const parts = (base + relative).split('/');
      const stack = [];
      for (const p of parts) {
        if (p === '..') stack.pop();
        else if (p !== '.' && p !== '') stack.push(p);
      }
      return '/' + stack.join('/');
    },

    /** Resuelve './X' o '../X' o '/abs' a un path absoluto. Sin extensión opcional. */
    resolve(importer, spec) {
      if (spec.startsWith('/')) return this._normalize(spec);
      const dir = importer.substring(0, importer.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
      if (spec.startsWith('./') || spec.startsWith('../')) {
        return this._normalize(this._join(dir, spec));
      }
      return null;
    },

    /** Resuelve y busca extensión si no la tiene. */
    resolveWithExt(importer, spec) {
      const resolved = this.resolve(importer, spec);
      if (!resolved) return null;
      if (this.ext(resolved)) return fs.exists(resolved) ? resolved : null;

      const candidates = [
        resolved + '.svelte',
        resolved + '.svelte.js',
        resolved + '.svelte.ts',
        resolved + '.js',
        resolved + '.ts',
        resolved + '.css',
        resolved + '.json',
        resolved + '/index.svelte',
        resolved + '/index.js',
      ];
      for (const c of candidates) {
        if (fs.exists(c)) return c;
      }
      return null;
    },

    ext(path) {
      const last = path.split('/').pop();
      const dot = last.lastIndexOf('.');
      return dot <= 0 ? '' : last.slice(dot + 1);
    },

    _normalize(path) {
      let out = path;
      while (out.includes('//')) out = out.replace(/\/\//g, '/');
      if (out.length > 1 && out[0] !== '/') out = '/' + out;
      return out;
    },
  };

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Hash rápido (djb2) para detectar cambios en caché
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  function hashOf(content) {
    let h = 5381;
    for (let i = 0; i < content.length; i++) {
      h = ((h << 5) + h + content.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
    }
    return 'h' + (h >>> 0).toString(36);
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // toDataUrl — codifica un string como data: URL
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  function toDataUrl(code) {
    // Antes: spread operator sobre Uint8Array → "Maximum call stack size exceeded"
    // para bundles > ~50KB. Acumulamos en string con loop.
    let bin = '';
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(code);
    const chunk = 8192;
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunk) {
      bin += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + chunk));
    }
    return 'data:text/javascript;base64,' + btoa(bin);
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Loaders — procesan cada extensión a un módulo
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const loaders = {
    /** @param {string} path @param {string} content */
    svelte(path, content) {
      try {
        const r = svelteCompile(content, { generate: 'client', filename: path, dev: false });
        return {
          ok: true,
          kind: 'svelte',
          js: r.js.code,
          css: r.css?.code || '',
          warnings: r.warnings || [],
        };
      } catch (e) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          kind: 'svelte',
          errors: [{
            file: path,
            message: e.message || String(e),
            start: e.start,
            position: e.position,
            frame: e.frame,
          }],
        };
      }
    },

    js(path, content) {
      return { ok: true, kind: 'js', js: content, css: '', warnings: [] };
    },

    /** Genera un módulo side-effect que inyecta un <style> en runtime. */
    css(path, content) {
      const escapedPath = JSON.stringify(path);
      const escapedCss = JSON.stringify(content);
      const js =
        `const __path=${escapedPath};const __css=${escapedCss};` +
        `if(typeof document!=='undefined'){` +
        `const __old=document.querySelector('style[data-repl-style="'+__path+'"]');` +
        `if(__old)__old.remove();` +
        `const __s=document.createElement('style');` +
        `__s.setAttribute('data-repl-style',__path);` +
        `__s.textContent=__css;` +
        `document.head.appendChild(__s);}` +
        `export default __css;`;
      return { ok: true, kind: 'css', js, css: '', warnings: [] };
    },

    json(path, content) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
        return { ok: true, kind: 'json', js: `export default ${JSON.stringify(parsed)};`, css: '', warnings: [] };
      } catch (e) {
        return {
          ok: false,
          kind: 'json',
          errors: [{ file: path, message: 'JSON inválido: ' + e.message, start: { line: e.lineNumber || 1, column: (e.columnNumber || 1) - 1 } }],
        };
      }
    },
  };

// (Función maskStringsAndComments removida — usamos parser AST)

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Parser — extracción de imports via AST real (acorn)
  // Cubre correctamente:
  //   - ImportDeclaration:   import X from './Y', import './Y'
  //   - ImportExpression:    import('./Y') (dynamic)
  //   - ExportNamed+source:  export { x } from './Y'
  //   - ExportAllDeclaration: export * from './Y'
  // Ignora correctamente strings, comentarios y template literals.
  // Devuelve [] si el código no parsea (no lanza).
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const parser = {
    _acorn: null,

    /** Carga acorn desde esm.sh. Llamar una vez al inicio. */
    async load() {
      if (this._acorn) return this._acorn;
      const m = await import('https://esm.sh/acorn@8.14.0');
      this._acorn = m.default || m;
      return this._acorn;
    },

    /** Parsea código JS y devuelve [{ spec, start, end, kind }].
     *  start/end son offsets absolutos sobre `code` original (NO sobre los strings
     *  de los sources; son los offsets del nodo AST). */
    extractImports(code) {
      if (!this._acorn) return [];
      let ast;
      try {
        ast = this._acorn.parse(code, { ecmaVersion: 'latest', sourceType: 'module' });
      } catch (e) {
        return [];
      }
      const out = [];
      const walk = (node) => {
        if (!node || typeof node !== 'object' || typeof node.type !== 'string') return;

        if (node.type === 'ImportDeclaration' && node.source && typeof node.source.value === 'string') {
          out.push({ spec: node.source.value, start: node.source.start, end: node.source.end, kind: 'static' });
        }
        else if (node.type === 'ImportExpression' && node.source && node.source.type === 'Literal' && typeof node.source.value === 'string') {
          out.push({ spec: node.source.value, start: node.source.start, end: node.source.end, kind: 'dynamic' });
        }
        else if (node.type === 'ExportNamedDeclaration' && node.source) {
          out.push({ spec: node.source.value, start: node.source.start, end: node.source.end, kind: 'reexport' });
        }
        else if (node.type === 'ExportAllDeclaration' && node.source) {
          out.push({ spec: node.source.value, start: node.source.start, end: node.source.end, kind: 'reexport' });
        }

        for (const key in node) {
          if (key === 'type' || key === 'start' || key === 'end' || key === 'loc' || key === 'range') continue;
          const v = node[key];
          if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach(walk);
          else if (v && typeof v === 'object' && typeof v.type === 'string') walk(v);
        }
      };
      walk(ast);
      return out;
    },
  };


  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const compiler = {
    _nodes: new Map(),  // path → { hash, deps: Set, compiled: { js, css, warnings } | null }
    /** Hook opcional para instrumentar builds (stress test). Se llama con
     *  { onFileCompile(path, ms, bytes) } antes del compile(). Pasar null
     *  para desactivar. */
    _instrument: null,

    /** Topological walk desde 'entry'. Devuelve { modules: {path → {js, css}}, errors: [...], warnings: [...], order: [paths] }. */
    build(entry = '/App.svelte') {
      const errors = [];
      const order = [];
      const visiting = new Set();
      const visited = new Set();
      const inst = this._instrument;

      const visit = (path) => {
        if (visited.has(path)) return;
        if (visiting.has(path)) {
          errors.push({ file: path, message: 'Import cíclico detectado: ' + path });
          return;
        }
        visiting.add(path);

        if (!fs.exists(path)) {
          errors.push({ file: path, message: 'Archivo no encontrado: ' + path });
          visiting.delete(path);
          return;
        }

        const content = fs.read(path);
        const hash = hashOf(content);
        let node = this._nodes.get(path);
        const stale = !node || node.hash !== hash;

        if (stale) {
          if (!node) {
            node = { hash: '', deps: new Set(), compiled: null };
            this._nodes.set(path, node);
          }
          const ext = resolver.ext(path);
          const loader = loaders[ext];
          if (!loader) {
            errors.push({ file: path, message: 'Extensión no soportada: ' + (ext || '(sin extensión)') });
            visiting.delete(path);
            return;
          }
          // Instrumentación opcional (solo stress test). Mide el tiempo de
          // la llamada al loader (svelteCompile) por archivo.
          let result;
          if (inst) {
            const t0 = performance.now();
            result = loader(path, content);
            inst.onFileCompile && inst.onFileCompile(path, performance.now() - t0, content.length);
          } else {
            result = loader(path, content);
          }
          if (!result.ok) {
            for (const err of result.errors) errors.push(err);
            node.compiled = null;
          } else {
            // Recolectar deps del código compilado (raw, antes de reescribir)
            const rawDeps = this._extractDeps(path, result.js);
            node.deps = new Set(rawDeps);
            node.compiled = result;
            node.hash = hash;
          }
        }

        // Visitar deps en orden topológico
        for (const dep of node.deps) {
          visit(dep);
        }

        visiting.delete(path);
        visited.add(path);
        order.push(path);
      };

      visit(entry);

      // Recolectar warnings de los módulos compilados
      const warnings = [];
      for (const path of order) {
        const node = this._nodes.get(path);
        if (node?.compiled?.warnings?.length) {
          for (const w of node.compiled.warnings) warnings.push({ file: path, ...w });
        }
      }

      // Construir módulos finales: ahora reescribimos imports a data URLs
      const modules = {};
      for (const path of order) {
        const node = this._nodes.get(path);
        if (!node?.compiled) continue;
        modules[path] = {
          js: this._rewriteImports(node.compiled.js, path),
          css: node.compiled.css || '',
          kind: node.compiled.kind,
        };
      }

      return { modules, errors, warnings };
    },

    /** Extrae las rutas que importa un módulo (después del loader, antes del rewrite).
     *  Usa el AST real (acorn). Solo imports estáticos relativos cuentan como deps. */
    _extractDeps(importer, code) {
      const deps = new Set();
      const imports = parser.extractImports(code);
      for (const { spec, kind } of imports) {
        if (kind !== 'static') continue;
        if (spec.startsWith('.') || spec.startsWith('/')) {
          const resolved = resolver.resolveWithExt(importer, spec);
          if (resolved) deps.add(resolved);
        }
      }
      return deps;
    },

    /** Reescribe imports en código compilado para que funcionen dentro de data: URLs.
     *  Usa offsets reales del AST (acorn), no regex. Aplicamos reemplazos
     *  derecha→izquierda para no invalidar offsets.
     *  Los offsets del AST en acorn para un Literal incluyen las comillas,
     *  por lo que preservamos el primer y último carácter (la comilla) y
     *  solo sustituimos el contenido del spec.
     *  - './X' / '../X' / '/X' → data: URL del módulo compilado (recursivo)
     *  - 'svelte/internal/...' → URL absoluta del runtime (tomada del import map)
     *  - Dynamic imports relativos también se reescriben (import('./X'))
     *  - Strings en comentarios o literales NO se tocan (el parser los ignora) */
    _rewriteImports(code, importer, depth = 0) {
      if (depth > 64) return code;
      const imports = parser.extractImports(code);
      let result = code;
      // Aplicar reemplazos de derecha a izquierda para no invalidar offsets.
      for (let i = imports.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const { spec, start, end, kind } = imports[i];
        let replacement = spec;
        // start/end apuntan a las comillas; preservarlas y reemplazar solo el spec.
        const quoteOpen = code[start];
        const quoteClose = code[end - 1];
        const specStart = start + 1;
        const specEnd = end - 1;

        const isRelative = spec.startsWith('.') || spec.startsWith('/');
        if (kind === 'dynamic' && isRelative) {
          const resolved = resolver.resolveWithExt(importer, spec);
          const node = resolved && this._nodes.get(resolved);
          if (node?.compiled) {
            replacement = quoteOpen + this._rewriteImports(node.compiled.js, resolved, depth + 1) + quoteClose;
            result = result.slice(0, start) + replacement + result.slice(end);
            continue;
          }
        } else if (kind === 'static' || kind === 'reexport') {
          if (isRelative) {
            const resolved = resolver.resolveWithExt(importer, spec);
            const node = resolved && this._nodes.get(resolved);
            if (node?.compiled) {
              replacement = toDataUrl(this._rewriteImports(node.compiled.js, resolved, depth + 1));
            }
          } else {
            replacement = runtime?.importMap?.imports?.[spec] || spec;
          }
        }
        // Mantener comillas; reemplazar solo el contenido del spec.
        result = result.slice(0, specStart) + replacement + result.slice(specEnd);
      }
      return result;
    },

    /** Para poner en data URL: el JS ya tiene sus propios imports a data URLs de primer nivel.
     * Pero los módulos inferiores también necesitan sus imports reescritos.
     * Como el compiler procesa todos los archivos, sus `compiled.js` ya tiene imports
     * hacia otros archivos; necesitamos reescribirlos recursivamente antes de meterlos
     * en data URL. Hacemos esto en build() en vez de aquí. */
    _stripImportsForUrl(code, importer) {
      return this._rewriteImports(code, importer);
    },

    /** Invalida un path y sus dependents. Llamar cuando el fs notifica un cambio. */
    invalidate(path) {
      const node = this._nodes.get(path);
      if (node) {
        node.hash = '';
        node.compiled = null;
      }
      // También invalidar dependents (los que importan este)
      for (const [otherPath, otherNode] of this._nodes) {
        if (otherNode.deps.has(path)) this.invalidate(otherPath);
      }
    },

    /** Limpia toda la caché (útil cuando cambia la versión de Svelte). */
    clear() {
      this._nodes.clear();
    },
  };

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // La carga del runtime se ha movido dentro de la clase Runtime.
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Preview — monta el bundle en el iframe sandbox
  // Lee runtime.importMap en cada mount (la referencia es perezosa).
  // Soporta dos tipos de bundle:
  //   - .svelte: bundle.entryJs → data URL + importMap + Bootstrap
  //   - .md / .schema.json: bundle.kind === 'html' → bundle.html ya es srcdoc
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const preview = {
    el: document.getElementById('preview'),

    mount(bundle) {
      if (bundle && bundle.kind === 'html') {
        this.el.srcdoc = bundle.html;
        return;
      }
      const entryDataUrl = toDataUrl(bundle.entryJs);
      this.el.srcdoc = buildSrcdoc(entryDataUrl, runtime.importMap);
    },

    reload() {
      const src = this.el.srcdoc;
      this.el.srcdoc = '';
      this.el.srcdoc = src;
    },
  };

  function buildSrcdoc(entryDataUrl, importMap) {
    const importMapJson = JSON.stringify(importMap || { imports: {} });
    return `<!doctype html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Preview</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.11.3/font/bootstrap-icons.min.css">
  <scr` + `ipt type="importmap">${importMapJson}</scr` + `ipt>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; }
    #root { padding: 1rem; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div id="root">cargando…</div>
  <scr` + `ipt type="module">
    window.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
      parent.postMessage({ type: 'iframe-error', error: e.message || String(e), line: e.lineno, col: e.colno }, '*');
    });
    window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (e) => {
      parent.postMessage({ type: 'iframe-error', error: (e.reason && e.reason.message) || String(e.reason) }, '*');
    });
    try {
      const mod = await import(${JSON.stringify(entryDataUrl)});
      const { mount } = await import('svelte');
      const root = document.getElementById('root');
      root.innerHTML = ''; // limpiar "cargando…" antes de montar (Svelte 5 no lo hace solo)
      const t0 = performance.now();
      mount(mod.default, { target: root });
      // Tiempo interno del iframe: desde el inicio del mount hasta que mount() retorna.
      // Es una buena aproximación del "tiempo de hidratación" (compilación + render inicial).
      parent.postMessage({ type: 'stress-hydrated', t: performance.now() - t0 }, '*');
      parent.postMessage({ type: 'iframe-ready' }, '*');
    } catch (e) {
      const msg = (e && e.message) || String(e);
      parent.postMessage({ type: 'iframe-error', error: 'Error al montar: ' + msg }, '*');
      const root = document.getElementById('root');
      root.innerHTML = '<pre style="color:red;padding:1rem;background:#fef2f2;border-radius:6px;white-space:pre-wrap">' + msg + '</pre>';
    }
  </scr` + `ipt>
</body>
</html>`;
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Generador de la prueba de rendimiento
  // Crea 50 componentes sintéticos con variedad extrema:
  //   10 micro (1-5 líneas)  · 20 small (5-30)  · 15 medium (30-150)  · 5 large (200-500)
  // Los patrones se eligen por índice, así cada archivo es único.
  // Se exporta como función para que `loadTemplate` la invoque al cargar
  // la plantilla "stress-50" — no inflamos el init con 50 strings estáticos.
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  function buildStressProject() {
    const SCR = '<scr' + 'ipt>';
    const END = '</' + 'script>';
    const files = {};

    // Paleta de colores determinista por índice
    const palette = ['#ff3e00','#10b981','#3b82f6','#f59e0b','#8b5cf6','#ec4899','#14b8a6','#ef4444','#6366f1','#84cc16'];
    const color = (i) => palette[i % palette.length];
    const cap = (s) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);

    // ───────────── 10 micro (1-5 líneas) ─────────────
    const microPatterns = [
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let n = $state(0);\n${END}\n\n<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick={() => n++}>M${i}: {n}</button>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { text = 'tag ' + ${i} } = $props();\n${END}\n\n<span class="badge" style="background:${color(i)}">${i} {text}</span>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let v = $state(true);\n${END}\n\n<span class="badge text-bg-\${v ? 'success' : 'secondary'}">{v ? 'ON' : 'OFF'} #${i}</span>\n<button class="btn btn-sm btn-link p-0 ms-1" onclick={() => v = !v}>↻</button>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let x = $state(${i * 7});\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-1 small">#${i}: <strong>{x}</strong></div>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { label = 'L${i}' } = $props();\n${END}\n\n<i class="bi bi-star-fill" style="color:${color(i)}"></i> <small>${i} {label}</small>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let n = $state(${i});\n${END}\n\n<span class="d-inline-block px-2 rounded text-white" style="background:${color(i)}">${i} · {n}</span>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let ok = $state(${i % 2 === 0});\n${END}\n\n<i class="bi bi-\${ok ? 'check-circle' : 'circle'}" style="color:${color(i)}"></i> #${i}`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let t = $state(${i * 13});\n${END}\n\n<small class="text-muted">${i}: {t.toFixed(0)}ms</small>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { w = ${i} } = $props();\n${END}\n\n<div style="width:{w}px;height:6px;background:${color(i)}"></div>`,
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let s = $state('m${i}');\n${END}\n\n<code class="small">${i}: {s}</code>`,
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      files['/stress/micro_' + String(i).padStart(2, '0') + '.svelte'] = microPatterns[i % microPatterns.length](i);
    }

    // ───────────── 20 small (5-30 líneas) ─────────────
    const smallPatterns = [
      // Contador con etiqueta
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let n = $state(0);\n  const label = 'Item ${i}';\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">\n  <span><strong>{label}</strong></span>\n  <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">\n    <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary" onclick={() => n--}>−</button>\n    <button class="btn btn-light" disabled>{n}</button>\n    <button class="btn btn-outline-primary" onclick={() => n++}>+</button>\n  </div>\n</div>`,
      // Badge clickable
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { count = ${i} } = $props();\n  let active = $state(false);\n${END}\n\n<button class="badge text-bg-\${active ? 'primary' : 'light'} border" onclick={() => active = !active}>\n  ${cap(color(i).slice(1))} {count} {active ? '✓' : ''}\n</button>`,
      // Input + eco
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let v = $state('${i}');\n${END}\n\n<div class="input-group input-group-sm">\n  <span class="input-group-text">#${i}</span>\n  <input class="form-control" bind:value={v} />\n</div>\n<small class="text-muted">Eco: {v}</small>`,
      // Toggle booleano
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let on = $state(${i % 2 === 0});\n${END}\n\n<div class="form-check form-switch">\n  <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="sw-${i}" bind:checked={on} />\n  <label class="form-check-label" for="sw-${i}">Switch ${i}</label>\n</div>`,
      // Slider con doble
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let v = $state(${i * 10});\n  const dbl = $derived(v * 2);\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  <label>${i}: {v} (x2 = {dbl})</label>\n  <input type="range" min="0" max="100" bind:value={v} class="form-range" />\n</div>`,
      // Lista mini
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let items = $state([${i}, ${i + 1}, ${i + 2}]);\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  #${i}:\n  {#each items as it}\n    <span class="badge text-bg-light me-1">{it}</span>\n  {/each}\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-link p-0" onclick={() => items = [...items, items.length ? items[items.length - 1] + 1 : 0]}>+</button>\n</div>`,
      // Card simple
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { title = 'Card ${i}', body = 'Body ${i}' } = $props();\n${END}\n\n<div class="card card-body py-2 px-3">\n  <strong class="small">#{${i}} {title}</strong>\n  <p class="small text-muted mb-0">{body}</p>\n</div>`,
      // Progress bar
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let pct = $state(${(i * 11) % 101});\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between"><span>Bar ${i}</span><span>{pct}%</span></div>\n  <div class="progress" style="height:6px"><div class="progress-bar" style="width:{pct}%;background:${color(i)}"></div></div>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-link p-0" onclick={() => pct = (pct + 10) % 110}>+10</button>\n</div>`,
      // Alert cerrable
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let show = $state(true);\n  const kind = ${i % 2 === 0 ? "'info'" : "'warning'"};\n${END}\n\n{#if show}\n<div class="alert alert-{kind} py-1 px-2 small d-flex justify-content-between">\n  <span>Alert ${i} ({kind})</span>\n  <button class="btn-close btn-close-sm" onclick={() => show = false}></button>\n</div>\n{/if}`,
      // Mini cronómetro
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let t = $state(0);\n  let on = $state(false);\n  $effect(() => {\n    if (!on) return;\n    const id = setInterval(() => t++, 1000);\n    return () => clearInterval(id);\n  });\n${END}\n\n<div class="small d-flex gap-2 align-items-center">\n  <span>⏱ ${i}: <strong>{t}s</strong></span>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary py-0" onclick={() => on = !on}>{on ? 'stop' : 'start'}</button>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary py-0" onclick={() => { t = 0; on = false; }}>rst</button>\n</div>`,
      // Etiqueta con derived
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let count = $state(${i * 3});\n  const parity = $derived(count % 2 === 0 ? 'par' : 'impar');\n${END}\n\n<div class="small d-flex gap-2">\n  <span class="badge text-bg-\${parity === 'par' ? 'success' : 'warning'}">#{${i}} {parity}</span>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary py-0" onclick={() => count--}>−</button>\n  <span>{count}</span>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary py-0" onclick={() => count++}>+</button>\n</div>`,
      // Texto + char count
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let s = $state('Texto ${i}');\n  const len = $derived(s.length);\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  <input class="form-control form-control-sm mb-1" bind:value={s} />\n  <span class="text-muted">${i}: {len} caracteres</span>\n</div>`,
      // Botón con icono
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let clicks = $state(0);\n${END}\n\n<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-${i % 2 === 0 ? 'primary' : 'danger'}" onclick={() => clicks++}>\n  <i class="bi bi-hand-thumbs-up"></i> ${i} {clicks}\n</button>`,
      // Tag removable
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let tags = $state(['a${i}', 'b${i}', 'c${i}']);\n  function remove(t) { tags = tags.filter(x => x !== t); }\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  Tags ${i}:\n  {#each tags as t}\n    <span class="badge text-bg-light border me-1">{t} <a href="#" onclick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); remove(t); }}>✕</a></span>\n  {/each}\n</div>`,
      // Range + output
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let r = $state(${i * 5});\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  <label>${i}: <strong style="color:${color(i)}">{r}</strong></label>\n  <input type="range" class="form-range" min="0" max="100" bind:value={r} />\n</div>`,
      // Píldora
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { text = 'pill ${i}' } = $props();\n${END}\n\n<span class="badge rounded-pill text-bg-${i % 3 === 0 ? 'primary' : i % 3 === 1 ? 'info' : 'secondary'}">{text}</span>`,
      // Anchor con contador
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let v = $state(0);\n${END}\n\n<a href="#" class="small" onclick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); v++; }}>\n  Link ${i} <span class="badge text-bg-light">{v}</span>\n</a>`,
      // Checkbox + label
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let items = $state([${i % 2 === 0}, ${i % 3 === 0}, ${i % 5 === 0}]);\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  {#each items as v, idx}\n    <div class="form-check">\n      <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="chk-${i}-\${idx}" bind:checked={items[idx]} />\n      <label class="form-check-label" for="chk-${i}-\${idx}">Opción ${i}.{idx}</label>\n    </div>\n  {/each}\n</div>`,
      // Select con derived
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let v = $state('${i}');\n  const opts = ['${i}', '${i + 1}', '${i + 2}'];\n${END}\n\n<div class="small d-flex gap-2 align-items-center">\n  <label>${i}:</label>\n  <select class="form-select form-select-sm w-auto" bind:value={v}>\n    {#each opts as o}<option value={o}>{o}</option>{/each}\n  </select>\n  <span class="text-muted">→ {v}</span>\n</div>`,
      // KPI card mini
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let { label = 'KPI ${i}', val = ${i * 7} } = $props();\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 text-center" style="border-color:${color(i)} !important">\n  <div class="small text-muted">{label}</div>\n  <div class="h5 mb-0" style="color:${color(i)}">{val}</div>\n</div>`,
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
      files['/stress/small_' + String(i).padStart(2, '0') + '.svelte'] = smallPatterns[i % smallPatterns.length](i);
    }

    // ───────────── 15 medium (30-150 líneas) ─────────────
    const mediumPatterns = [
      // Tabs internos
      (i) => {
        const tabs = ['Inicio', 'Datos', 'Ajustes', 'Histórico'];
        const bodies = [
          'Vista principal del panel #' + i + '.',
          'Aquí verás los datos del día ' + i + '.',
          'Configuración persistente del usuario ' + i + '.',
          'Últimos ' + (i + 5) + ' eventos registrados.',
        ];
        return `${SCR}\n  let active = $state(0);\n  const tabs = ${JSON.stringify(tabs)};\n  const bodies = ${JSON.stringify(bodies)};\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded">\n  <ul class="nav nav-tabs nav-sm">\n    {#each tabs as t, idx}\n      <li class="nav-item">\n        <button class="nav-link {active === idx ? 'active' : ''}" onclick={() => active = idx}>{t}</button>\n      </li>\n    {/each}\n  </ul>\n  <div class="p-3 small">\n    {#each tabs as _, idx}\n      {#if active === idx}<div>{bodies[idx]}</div>{/if}\n    {/each}\n  </div>\n  <div class="px-3 pb-2 small text-muted">Tabla ${i} · activa: <strong>{tabs[active]}</strong></div>\n</div>`;
      },
      // Tabla ordenable
      (i) => {
        const rows = [];
        for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) rows.push({ id: r + 1, name: 'Item ' + (i * 10 + r), value: (i + 1) * (r + 3) });
        return `${SCR}\n  const initial = ${JSON.stringify(rows)};\n  let rows = $state(initial);\n  let sortKey = $state('id');\n  let sortDir = $state(1);\n  const sorted = $derived([...rows].sort((a, b) => sortDir * (a[sortKey] - b[sortKey])));\n  function sort(k) {\n    if (sortKey === k) sortDir = -sortDir;\n    else { sortKey = k; sortDir = 1; }\n  }\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded">\n  <div class="px-2 py-1 small fw-bold bg-light border-bottom">Tabla ${i} · {rows.length} filas</div>\n  <table class="table table-sm mb-0 small">\n    <thead><tr>\n      <th onclick={() => sort('id')} style="cursor:pointer">ID {sortKey === 'id' ? (sortDir > 0 ? '↑' : '↓') : ''}</th>\n      <th>Nombre</th>\n      <th onclick={() => sort('value')} style="cursor:pointer">Valor {sortKey === 'value' ? (sortDir > 0 ? '↑' : '↓') : ''}</th>\n    </tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n      {#each sorted as r}\n        <tr><td>{r.id}</td><td>{r.name}</td><td>{r.value}</td></tr>\n      {/each}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n</div>`;
      },
      // Filtro de búsqueda
      (i) => {
        const all = [];
        for (let r = 0; r < 12; r++) all.push('Elemento ' + (i * 100 + r));
        return `${SCR}\n  let q = $state('');\n  const all = ${JSON.stringify(all)};\n  const filtered = $derived(all.filter(x => x.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())));\n  const count = $derived(filtered.length);\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2">\n  <input class="form-control form-control-sm mb-2" placeholder="Buscar en ${i}…" bind:value={q} />\n  <div class="small text-muted">{count} / {all.length} resultados</div>\n  <ul class="list-unstyled mb-0 small" style="max-height:120px;overflow:auto">\n    {#each filtered as x}<li class="py-0">· {x}</li>{/each}\n    {#if filtered.length === 0}<li class="text-muted fst-italic">sin resultados</li>{/if}\n  </ul>\n</div>`;
      },
      // Wizard 3 pasos
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let step = $state(0);\n  const steps = ['Datos básicos', 'Preferencias', 'Confirmación'];\n  const fields = [\n    ['Nombre', 'Email'],\n    ['Tema', 'Idioma'],\n    ['Resumen', 'Aceptar'],\n  ];\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2">\n  <div class="small text-muted mb-1">Wizard ${i} · paso {step + 1} / {steps.length}</div>\n  <div class="progress mb-2" style="height:6px"><div class="progress-bar" style="width:{(step + 1) / steps.length * 100}%"></div></div>\n  <div class="mb-2"><strong>{steps[step]}</strong></div>\n  <div class="row g-1 small mb-2">\n    {#each fields[step] as f}<div class="col-6"><input class="form-control form-control-sm" placeholder={f} /></div>{/each}\n  </div>\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between">\n    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" disabled={step === 0} onclick={() => step--}>Atrás</button>\n    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" disabled={step === steps.length - 1} onclick={() => step++}>Siguiente</button>\n  </div>\n</div>`,
      // Accordion

          { title: '¿Qué es SIVO App Studio?', body: 'Constructor visual en el navegador para crear apps sin instalar nada.' },

        ];
        return `${SCR}\n  let open = $state(0);\n  const items = ${JSON.stringify(items)};\n${END}\n\n<div class="accordion accordion-sm" id="acc-${i}">\n  {#each items as it, idx}\n    <div class="accordion-item">\n      <h2 class="accordion-header">\n        <button class="accordion-button {open === idx ? '' : 'collapsed'} small" type="button" onclick={() => open = open === idx ? -1 : idx}>\n          {it.title}\n        </button>\n      </h2>\n      {#if open === idx}\n        <div class="accordion-body small">{it.body}</div>\n      {/if}\n    </div>\n  {/each}\n</div>`;
      },
      // Star rating
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let val = $state(${i % 5});\n  let hover = $state(-1);\n  const display = $derived(hover >= 0 ? hover : val);\n${END}\n\n<div class="small">\n  Rating ${i}:\n  {#each [0, 1, 2, 3, 4] as n}\n    <i\n      class="bi bi-star{n < display ? '-fill' : ''}"\n      style="color: {n < display ? '${color(i)}' : '#ccc'}; cursor: pointer; font-size: 1.1rem"\n      onmouseenter={() => hover = n}\n      onmouseleave={() => hover = -1}\n      onclick={() => val = n}\n    ></i>\n  {/each}\n  <span class="ms-1 text-muted">({val + 1}/5)</span>\n</div>`,
      // Multi-step slider
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let a = $state(${i * 4});\n  let b = $state(${i * 4 + 30});\n  const sum = $derived(a + b);\n  const diff = $derived(Math.abs(a - b));\n  const avg = $derived((a + b) / 2);\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between"><span>${i}: A={a}</span><span>B={b}</span></div>\n  <input type="range" class="form-range" min="0" max="100" bind:value={a} />\n  <input type="range" class="form-range" min="0" max="100" bind:value={b} />\n  <div class="d-flex gap-3 text-muted">\n    <span>Σ {sum}</span><span>|Δ| {diff}</span><span>x̄ {avg.toFixed(1)}</span>\n  </div>\n</div>`,
      // Lista con búsqueda y selección múltiple
      (i) => {
        const all = [];
        for (let r = 0; r < 10; r++) all.push('Opción ' + (i * 10 + r));
        return `${SCR}\n  let q = $state('');\n  let selected = $state(new Set());\n  const all = ${JSON.stringify(all)};\n  const filtered = $derived(all.filter(x => x.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())));\n  function toggle(x) {\n    if (selected.has(x)) selected.delete(x);\n    else selected.add(x);\n    selected = new Set(selected);\n  }\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  <input class="form-control form-control-sm mb-1" placeholder="Buscar ${i}…" bind:value={q} />\n  <div class="text-muted mb-1">{selected.size} seleccionados</div>\n  <div style="max-height:130px;overflow:auto">\n    {#each filtered as x}\n      <div class="form-check">\n        <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="ms-${i}-{x}" checked={selected.has(x)} onchange={() => toggle(x)} />\n        <label class="form-check-label" for="ms-${i}-{x}">{x}</label>\n      </div>\n    {/each}\n  </div>\n</div>`;
      },
      // Kanban column simple
      (i) => {

      },
      // Cronómetro doble con efecto
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let a = $state(0);\n  let b = $state(0);\n  let runA = $state(false);\n  let runB = $state(false);\n  $effect(() => {\n    if (!runA) return;\n    const id = setInterval(() => a++, 100);\n    return () => clearInterval(id);\n  });\n  $effect(() => {\n    if (!runB) return;\n    const id = setInterval(() => b++, 100);\n    return () => clearInterval(id);\n  });\n  const fastest = $derived(a < b ? 'A' : a > b ? 'B' : 'empate');\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  <div class="row g-2">\n    <div class="col-6 text-center">\n      <div>A ${i}</div>\n      <div class="h5" style="color:${color(i)}">{(a / 10).toFixed(1)}s</div>\n      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick={() => runA = !runA}>{runA ? 'stop' : 'go'}</button>\n      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" onclick={() => { a = 0; runA = false; }}>rst</button>\n    </div>\n    <div class="col-6 text-center">\n      <div>B ${i}</div>\n      <div class="h5" style="color:${color(i + 3)}">{(b / 10).toFixed(1)}s</div>\n      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick={() => runB = !runB}>{runB ? 'stop' : 'go'}</button>\n      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" onclick={() => { b = 0; runB = false; }}>rst</button>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n  <div class="text-center text-muted mt-1">Más rápido: <strong>{fastest}</strong></div>\n</div>`,
      // Gráfico de barras manual

'${color(i)}' : '${color(i + 2)}'};height:{v / max * 100}%;cursor:pointer;transition:height .2s"\n      ></div>\n    {/each}\n  </div>\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between text-muted" style="font-size:10px">\n    {#each labels as l}<span>{l}</span>{/each}\n  </div>\n  {#if selected >= 0}<div class="mt-1">{labels[selected]} = <strong>{vals[selected]}</strong></div>{/if}\n</div>`;
      },
      // Color picker con preview
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let r = $state(${(i * 31) % 256});\n  let g = $state(${(i * 17) % 256});\n  let b = $state(${(i * 47) % 256});\n  const hex = $derived('#' + [r, g, b].map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(''));\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  Color ${i}:\n  <div class="d-flex gap-1 align-items-center mb-1">\n    <div style="width:40px;height:24px;background:{hex};border:1px solid #ccc;border-radius:3px"></div>\n    <code>{hex}</code>\n  </div>\n  <div class="d-flex gap-1 align-items-center">R<input type="range" min="0" max="255" bind:value={r} class="form-range" />{r}</div>\n  <div class="d-flex gap-1 align-items-center">G<input type="range" min="0" max="255" bind:value={g} class="form-range" />{g}</div>\n  <div class="d-flex gap-1 align-items-center">B<input type="range" min="0" max="255" bind:value={b} class="form-range" />{b}</div>\n</div>`,
      // Lista virtual simple
      (i) => {
        const items = [];
        for (let r = 0; r < 200; r++) items.push('Fila ' + (i * 1000 + r));
        return `${SCR}\n  const all = ${JSON.stringify(items)};\n  let offset = $state(0);\n  const pageSize = 12;\n  const visible = $derived(all.slice(offset, offset + pageSize));\n  const hasMore = $derived(offset + pageSize < all.length);\n  const hasPrev = $derived(offset > 0);\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  Virtual ${i} · mostrando {offset + 1}–{Math.min(offset + pageSize, all.length)} de {all.length}\n  <ul class="list-unstyled mb-1" style="height:160px;overflow:auto">\n    {#each visible as v}<li>· {v}</li>{/each}\n  </ul>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" disabled={!hasPrev} onclick={() => offset = Math.max(0, offset - pageSize)}>‹</button>\n  <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" disabled={!hasMore} onclick={() => offset += pageSize}>›</button>\n</div>`;
      },
      // Tag input editable
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let input = $state('');\n  let tags = $state(['svelte', 'compiler', 'test']);\n  function add() {\n    const v = input.trim();\n    if (v && !tags.includes(v)) tags = [...tags, v];\n    input = '';\n  }\n  function remove(t) { tags = tags.filter(x => x !== t); }\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  Tags ${i}:\n  <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-1">\n    {#each tags as t}\n      <span class="badge text-bg-${i % 2 === 0 ? 'primary' : 'info'}">{t} <a href="#" onclick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); remove(t); }} style="color:white;text-decoration:none">×</a></span>\n    {/each}\n  </div>\n  <div class="input-group input-group-sm">\n    <input class="form-control" placeholder="Añadir tag" bind:value={input} onkeydown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && add()} />\n    <button class="btn btn-outline-primary" onclick={add}>+</button>\n  </div>\n</div>`,
      // Calendar mini
      (i) => `${SCR}\n  let day = $state(${i % 28 + 1});\n  const monthDays = 30;\n  const monthName = ${i % 12};\n  const events = ${JSON.stringify(['Reunión ' + i, 'Review ' + i, 'Demo ' + i])};\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-2 small">\n  Mes ${i} · día <strong>{day}</strong> / {monthDays}\n  <div class="row g-1 my-1" style="font-size:10px">\n    {#each Array(monthDays) as _, d}\n      <div class="col p-0 px-1">\n        <button\n          class="btn btn-sm p-0 w-100 {day === d + 1 ?     ];
    for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
      files['/stress/medium_' + String(i).padStart(2, '0') + '.svelte'] = mediumPatterns[i % mediumPatterns.length](i);
    }

    // ───────────── 5 large (200-500 líneas) ─────────────
    // Widget 1: Dashboard resumen con 4 KPIs + lista de actividad + pequeño chart
    files['/stress/large_00.svelte'] = `${SCR}\n  // Dashboard resumen 0 — múltiples $derived, $effect, lista, búsqueda\n  let visitors = $state(0 * 123 + 456);\n  let orders = $state(0 * 17 + 89);\n  let revenue = $state(0 * 1234 + 5000);\n  let conversion = $state(0 / 100);\n  const total = $derived(visitors + orders + revenue + conversion);\n  const avgTicket = $derived(orders > 0 ? revenue / orders : 0);\n  let search = $state('');\n  const activities = $state([\n    { id: 1, who: 'Ana', what: 'compró 3 entradas', when: 'hace 2m' },\n    { id: 2, who: 'Luis', what: 'se registró', when: 'hace 5m' },\n    { id: 3, who: 'Marta', what: 'actualizó perfil', when: 'hace 12m' },\n    { id: 4, who: 'Pedro', what: 'comentó en el blog', when: 'hace 24m' },\n    { id: 5, who: 'Sara', what: 'invitó a un amigo', when: 'hace 1h' },\n    { id: 6, who: 'Diego', what: 'cerró sesión', when: 'hace 2h' },\n  ]);\n  const filtered = $derived(\n    search\n      ? activities.filter(a =>\n          (a.who + ' ' + a.what).toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())\n        )\n      : activities\n  );\n  let autoRefresh = $state(false);\n  $effect(() => {\n    if (!autoRefresh) return;\n    const id = setInterval(() => {\n      visitors += Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);\n      orders += Math.random() < 0.3 ? 1 : 0;\n      revenue += Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);\n    }, 2000);\n    return () => clearInterval(id);\n  });\n  const sparkData = $derived([10, 22, 18, 30, 28, 35, 40, 38, 45, 50, 48, 55].map(v => v + 0));\n  const sparkMax = $derived(Math.max(...sparkData));\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-3">\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">\n    <h6 class="mb-0">Dashboard #0</h6>\n    <div class="form-check form-switch m-0">\n      <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="ar-0" bind:checked={autoRefresh} />\n      <label class="form-check-label small" for="ar-0">Auto-refresh</label>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n  <div class="row g-2 mb-3">\n    <div class="col-3"><div class="border rounded p-2 text-center small"><div class="text-muted">Visitas</div><div class="h5 mb-0">${'${color(0)}'.replace('#', '')}</div></div></div>\n    <div class="col-3"><div class="border rounded p-2 text-center small"><div class="text-muted">Pedidos</div><div class="h5 mb-0">{orders}</div></div></div>\n    <div class="col-3"><div class="border rounded p-2 text-center small"><div class="text-muted">Ingresos</div><div class="h5 mb-0">{revenue}€</div></div></div>\n    <div class="col-3"><div class="border rounded p-2 text-center small"><div class="text-muted">Conversión</div><div class="h5 mb-0">{(conversion * 100).toFixed(1)}%</div></div></div>\n  </div>\n  <div class="mb-2 small">\n    <strong>Ticket medio:</strong> {avgTicket.toFixed(2)}€ · <strong>Total combinado:</strong> {total.toFixed(0)}\n  </div>\n  <div class="mb-2">\n    <div class="small text-muted">Tendencia</div>\n    <div class="d-flex align-items-end gap-1" style="height:36px">\n      {#each sparkData as v, idx}<div style="flex:1;background:${'${color(0)}'};height:{v / sparkMax * 100}%;opacity:{(idx + 1) / sparkData.length}"></div>{/each}\n    </div>\n  </div>\n  <div class="mb-2">\n    <input class="form-control form-control-sm" placeholder="Buscar actividad…" bind:value={search} />\n  </div>\n  <ul class="list-group list-group-flush" style="max-height:140px;overflow:auto">\n    {#each filtered as a}\n      <li class="list-group-item small px-0 py-1">\n        <strong>{a.who}</strong> {a.what} <span class="text-muted float-end">{a.when}</span>\n      </li>\n    {/each}\n    {#if filtered.length === 0}<li class="list-group-item small text-muted">sin resultados</li>{/if}\n  </ul>\n</div>`;

    // Widget 2: Tabla de datos con sort + filtro + paginación
    files['/stress/large_01.svelte'] = (() => {
      const headers = ['ID', 'Cliente', 'Producto', 'Cantidad', 'Precio', 'Estado'];
      const estados = ['pendiente', 'enviado', 'entregado', 'cancelado'];
      const productos = ['Mesa', 'Silla', 'Lámpara', 'Sofá', 'Estantería', 'Mesa auxiliar', 'Banco'];
      const initial = [];
      for (let r = 0; r < 35; r++) {
        initial.push({
          id: r + 1,
          cliente: 'Cliente ' + String.fromCharCode(65 + (r % 26)) + (r % 99),
          producto: productos[r % productos.length],
          cantidad: ((r * 7) % 10) + 1,
          precio: ((r * 13) % 200) + 20,
          estado: estados[r % estados.length],
        });
      }
      const initialJson = JSON.stringify(initial);
      return `${SCR}\n  const initial = ${initialJson};\n  const headers = ${JSON.stringify(headers)};\n  const estados = ${JSON.stringify(estados)};\n  let rows = $state(initial);\n  let sortKey = $state('id');\n  let sortDir = $state(1);\n  let filterEstado = $state('todos');\n  let q = $state('');\n  let page = $state(0);\n  const pageSize = 8;\n  const filtered = $derived(\n    rows.filter(r =>\n      (filterEstado === 'todos' || r.estado === filterEstado) &&\n      (q === '' || (r.cliente + ' ' + r.producto).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))\n    )\n  );\n  const sorted = $derived(\n    [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {\n      const av = a[sortKey];\n      const bv = b[sortKey];\n      if (typeof av === 'number') return sortDir * (av - bv);\n      return sortDir * String(av).localeCompare(String(bv));\n    })\n  );\n  const pages = $derived(Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sorted.length / pageSize)));\n  const visible = $derived(sorted.slice(page * pageSize, (page + 1) * pageSize));\n  const totalSum = $derived(filtered.reduce((s, r) => s + r.precio * r.cantidad, 0));\n  function sort(k) {\n    if (sortKey === k) sortDir = -sortDir;\n    else { sortKey = k; sortDir = 1; }\n    page = 0;\n  }\n  function setEstado(v) { filterEstado = v; page = 0; }\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded">\n  <div class="px-3 py-2 bg-light border-bottom d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">\n    <strong>Pedidos (${'${0}'})</strong>\n    <span class="small text-muted">{filtered.length} visibles · Total {totalSum.toFixed(2)}€</span>\n  </div>\n  <div class="p-2 d-flex gap-2 flex-wrap small">\n    <input class="form-control form-control-sm" placeholder="Buscar…" bind:value={q} style="max-width:200px" />\n    <select class="form-select form-select-sm" value={filterEstado} onchange={(e) => setEstado(e.target.value)} style="max-width:160px">\n      <option value="todos">Todos los estados</option>\n      {#each estados as e}<option value={e}>{e}</option>{/each}\n    </select>\n  </div>\n  <table class="table table-sm mb-0 small">\n    <thead class="table-light"><tr>\n      {#each headers as h, idx}\n        <th style="cursor:pointer" onclick={() => sort(['id','cliente','producto','cantidad','precio','estado'][idx])}>\n          {h} {sortKey === ['id','cliente','producto','cantidad','precio','estado'][idx] ? (sortDir > 0 ? '↑' : '↓') : ''}\n        </th>\n      {/each}\n    </tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n      {#each visible as r}\n        <tr>\n          <td>{r.id}</td><td>{r.cliente}</td><td>{r.producto}</td>\n          <td class="text-end">{r.cantidad}</td>\n          <td class="text-end">{r.precio}€</td>\n          <td><span class="badge text-bg-{r.estado === 'entregado' ? 'success' : r.estado === 'enviado' ? 'info' : r.estado === 'cancelado' ? 'danger' : 'warning'}">{r.estado}</span></td>\n        </tr>\n      {/each}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  <div class="px-3 py-2 border-top d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center small">\n    <span>Página {page + 1} / {pages}</span>\n    <div>\n      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" disabled={page === 0} onclick={() => page--}>‹</button>\n      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" disabled={page >= pages - 1} onclick={() => page++}>›</button>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</div>`;
    })();

    // Widget 3: Todo app completa
    files['/stress/large_02.svelte'] = (() => {
      const initial = [];
      for (let r = 0; r < 10; r++) {
        initial.push({ id: r + 1, text: 'Tarea #' + (r + 1), done: r % 3 === 0, priority: r % 3 });
      }
      return `${SCR}\n  let items = $state(${JSON.stringify(initial)});\n  let text = $state('');\n  let nextId = $state(${initial.length + 1});\n  let filter = $state('all');  // 'all' | 'active' | 'done'\n  let sortBy = $state('id');   // 'id' | 'priority' | 'text'\n  function add() {\n    const t = text.trim();\n    if (!t) return;\n    items = [...items, { id: nextId, text: t, done: false, priority: 0 }];\n    nextId++;\n    text = '';\n  }\n  function toggle(id) {\n    items = items.map(it => it.id === id ? { ...it, done: !it.done } : it);\n  }\n  function remove(id) {\n    items = items.filter(it => it.id !== id);\n  }\n  function setPriority(id, p) {\n    items = items.map(it => it.id === id ? { ...it, priority: p } : it);\n  }\n  const filtered = $derived(\n    filter === 'active' ? items.filter(i => !i.done)\n    : filter === 'done' ? items.filter(i => i.done)\n    : items\n  );\n  const sorted = $derived(\n    [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {\n      if (sortBy === 'priority') return b.priority - a.priority;\n      if (sortBy === 'text') return a.text.localeCompare(b.text);\n      return a.id - b.id;\n    })\n  );\n  const remaining = $derived(items.filter(i => !i.done).length);\n  const completed = $derived(items.filter(i => i.done).length);\n  const pct = $derived(items.length > 0 ? (completed / items.length) * 100 : 0);\n  $effect(() => {\n    if (items.length === 0) return;\n    const t = setTimeout(() => {}, 0); // noop, solo ejercita el effect\n    return () => clearTimeout(t);\n  });\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-3">\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">\n    <h6 class="mb-0">Mis tareas</h6>\n    <span class="small text-muted">{remaining} pendientes · {completed} hechas</span>\n  </div>\n  <div class="progress mb-2" style="height:6px"><div class="progress-bar bg-success" style="width:{pct}%"></div></div>\n  <form class="input-group input-group-sm mb-2" onsubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); add(); }}>\n    <input class="form-control" placeholder="Nueva tarea" bind:value={text} />\n    <button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit" disabled={!text.trim()}>Añadir</button>\n  </form>\n  <div class="d-flex gap-2 mb-2 small">\n    <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">\n      <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary {filter === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick={() => filter = 'all'}>Todas ({items.length})</button>\n      <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary {filter === 'active' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick={() => filter = 'active'}>Pendientes ({remaining})</button>\n      <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary {filter === 'done' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick={() => filter = 'done'}>Hechas ({completed})</button>\n    </div>\n    <select class="form-select form-select-sm w-auto" bind:value={sortBy}>\n      <option value="id">Orden: creación</option>\n      <option value="priority">Orden: prioridad</option>\n      <option value="text">Orden: alfabético</option>\n    </select>\n  </div>\n  <ul class="list-group list-group-flush">\n    {#each sorted as it (it.id)}\n      <li class="list-group-item px-0 py-1 d-flex align-items-center gap-2 small">\n        <input class="form-check-input mt-0" type="checkbox" checked={it.done} onchange={() => toggle(it.id)} />\n        <span style="flex:1;{it.done ? 'text-decoration:line-through;color:#888' : ''}">{it.text}</span>\n        <select class="form-select form-select-sm w-auto" value={it.priority} onchange={(e) => setPriority(it.id, +e.target.value)}>\n          <option value={0}>·</option>\n          <option value={1}>↑</option>\n          <option value={2}>↑↑</option>\n        </select>\n        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger py-0" onclick={() => remove(it.id)}>✕</button>\n      </li>\n    {/each}\n    {#if sorted.length === 0}<li class="list-group-item small text-muted fst-italic px-0">sin tareas</li>{/if}\n  </ul>\n</div>`;
    })();

    // Widget 4: Calendar con vista mensual
    files['/stress/large_03.svelte'] = (() => {
      const events = [];
      for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) events.push({ day: ((r * 3) % 28) + 1, title: 'Evento ' + (r + 1) });
      const eventJson = JSON.stringify(events);
      return `${SCR}\n  const monthNames = ['Enero','Febrero','Marzo','Abril','Mayo','Junio','Julio','Agosto','Septiembre','Octubre','Noviembre','Diciembre'];\n  let month = $state(3 % 12);\n  let year = $state(2024 + 3 % 2);\n  let selected = $state(1);\n  const events = $state(${eventJson});\n  const daysInMonth = $derived(new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate());\n  const firstWeekday = $derived(new Date(year, month, 1).getDay());\n  const cells = $derived(() => {\n    const out = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < firstWeekday; i++) out.push(null);\n    for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) out.push(d);\n    while (out.length % 7 !== 0) out.push(null);\n    return out;\n  });\n  const dayEvents = $derived(\n    (day) => events.filter(e => e.day === day && e.month === month).concat(\n      events.filter(e => e.day === day && !e.month).map(e => ({ ...e, month }))\n    )\n  );\n  const monthEvents = $derived(events.filter(e => !e.month || e.month === month));\n  function prev() {\n    if (month === 0) { month = 11; year--; } else month--;\n  }\n  function next() {\n    if (month === 11) { month = 0; year++; } else month++;\n  }\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-3">\n  <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">\n    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" onclick={prev}>‹</button>\n    <h6 class="mb-0">{monthNames[month]} {year}</h6>\n    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" onclick={next}>›</button>\n  </div>\n  <div class="row g-0 mb-1 small text-muted text-center">\n    {#each ['D','L','M','X','J','V','S'] as d}<div class="col border-bottom py-1">{d}</div>{/each}\n  </div>\n  <div class="row g-0" style="font-size:11px">\n    {#each cells() as d, i}\n      {#if d === null}\n        <div class="col border p-1 text-center text-muted" style="height:36px"></div>\n      {:else}\n        <div\n          class="col border p-1 text-center {selected === d ? 'bg-primary text-white' : ''}"\n          style="height:36px;cursor:pointer;{dayEvents(d).length > 0 ? 'font-weight:bold' : ''}"\n          onclick={() => selected = d}\n        >\n          {d}\n          {#if dayEvents(d).length > 0}<div style="font-size:8px">{dayEvents(d).length} ev</div>{/if}\n        </div>\n      {/if}\n    {/each}\n  </div>\n  <div class="mt-2 small">\n    <strong>Día {selected}:</strong>\n    {#if dayEvents(selected).length === 0}\n      <span class="text-muted fst-italic">sin eventos</span>\n    {:else}\n      <ul class="list-unstyled mb-0">\n        {#each dayEvents(selected) as e}<li>📌 {e.title}</li>{/each}\n      </ul>\n    {/if}\n  </div>\n  <div class="mt-2 small text-muted">Total eventos del mes: {monthEvents.length}</div>\n</div>`;
    })();

    // Widget 5: Form builder con validación
    files['/stress/large_04.svelte'] = `${SCR}\n  // Form builder completo\n  let form = $state({\n    nombre: '',\n    email: '',\n    edad: 25,\n    pais: 'es',\n    newsletter: true,\n    intereses: new Set(['tech']),\n    bio: '',\n  });\n  let touched = $state({});\n  let submitting = $state(false);\n  let sent = $state(false);\n  const errors = $derived(() => {\n    const e = {};\n    if (!form.nombre.trim()) e.nombre = 'El nombre es obligatorio';\n    else if (form.nombre.length < 2) e.nombre = 'Mínimo 2 caracteres';\n    if (!form.email.includes('@')) e.email = 'Email no válido';\n    if (form.edad < 18) e.edad = 'Debes ser mayor de edad';\n    if (form.edad > 120) e.edad = 'Edad no válida';\n    if (form.bio.length > 200) e.bio = 'Máximo 200 caracteres';\n    if (form.intereses.size === 0) e.intereses = 'Selecciona al menos uno';\n    return e;\n  });\n  const isValid = $derived(Object.keys(errors()).length === 0);\n  const progress = $derived(Math.round(\n    (['nombre','email','edad','pais','bio'].filter(k => form[k] && (!errors()[k])).length / 5) * 100\n  ));\n  function toggleInteres(t) {\n    if (form.intereses.has(t)) form.intereses.delete(t);\n    else form.intereses.add(t);\n    form.intereses = new Set(form.intereses);\n  }\n  function submit() {\n    touched = { nombre: true, email: true, edad: true, intereses: true, bio: true };\n    if (!isValid) return;\n    submitting = true;\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      submitting = false;\n      sent = true;\n    }, 600);\n  }\n  function reset() {\n    form = { nombre: '', email: '', edad: 25, pais: 'es', newsletter: true, intereses: new Set(['tech']), bio: '' };\n    touched = {};\n    sent = false;\n  }\n  const interesesOpts = ['tech', 'diseño', 'marketing', 'finanzas', 'educación'];\n  const paises = [['es', 'España'], ['mx', 'México'], ['ar', 'Argentina'], ['co', 'Colombia'], ['cl', 'Chile']];\n${END}\n\n<div class="border rounded p-3">\n  {#if sent}\n    <div class="text-center py-3">\n      <div class="display-6">✓</div>\n      <h5>¡Gracias, {form.nombre}!</h5>\n      <p class="small text-muted">Hemos registrado tu email ({form.email}) con {form.intereses.size} intereses.</p>\n      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick={reset}>Nuevo registro</button>\n    </div>\n  {:else}\n    <h6 class="mb-2">Registro</h6>\n    <div class="progress mb-3" style="height:6px"><div class="progress-bar" style="width:{progress}%"></div></div>\n    <div class="mb-2">\n      <label class="form-label small">Nombre *</label>\n      <input class="form-control form-control-sm {touched.nombre && errors().nombre ? 'is-invalid' : ''}" bind:value={form.nombre} onblur={() => touched.nombre = true} />\n      {#if touched.nombre && errors().nombre}<div class="invalid-feedback d-block small">{errors().nombre}</div>{/if}\n    </div>\n    <div class="mb-2">\n      <label class="form-label small">Email *</label>\n      <input type="email" class="form-control form-control-sm {touched.email && errors().email ? 'is-invalid' : ''}" bind:value={form.email} onblur={() => touched.email = true} />\n      {#if touched.email && errors().email}<div class="invalid-feedback d-block small">{errors().email}</div>{/if}\n    </div>\n    <div class="row g-2 mb-2">\n      <div class="col-6">\n        <label class="form-label small">Edad *</label>\n        <input type="number" class="form-control form-control-sm" bind:value={form.edad} />\n        {#if touched.edad && errors().edad}<div class="text-danger small">{errors().edad}</div>{/if}\n      </div>\n      <div class="col-6">\n        <label class="form-label small">País</label>\n        <select class="form-select form-select-sm" bind:value={form.pais}>\n          {#each paises as [code, name]}<option value={code}>{name}</option>{/each}\n        </select>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    <div class="mb-2">\n      <label class="form-label small d-block">Intereses *</label>\n      {#each interesesOpts as t}\n        <div class="form-check form-check-inline">\n          <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="int-4-{t}" checked={form.intereses.has(t)} onchange={() => toggleInteres(t)} />\n          <label class="form-check-label small" for="int-4-{t}">{t}</label>\n        </div>\n      {/each}\n      {#if touched.intereses && errors().intereses}<div class="text-danger small">{errors().intereses}</div>{/if}\n    </div>\n    <div class="mb-2">\n      <label class="form-label small">Bio ({form.bio.length}/200)</label>\n      <textarea class="form-control form-control-sm" rows="2" bind:value={form.bio} maxlength="200"></textarea>\n      {#if touched.bio && errors().bio}<div class="text-danger small">{errors().bio}</div>{/if}\n    </div>\n    <div class="form-check mb-3">\n      <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" id="nl-4" bind:checked={form.newsletter} />\n      <label class="form-check-label small" for="nl-4">Quiero recibir novedades</label>\n    </div>\n    <div class="d-flex gap-2">\n      <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" disabled={!isValid || submitting} onclick={submit}>{submitting ? 'Enviando…' : 'Enviar'}</button>\n      <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary btn-sm" onclick={reset}>Limpiar</button>\n    </div>\n  {/if}\n</div>`;

    // ───────────── utils.js compartido ─────────────
    files['/stress/utils.js'] = `// Helpers compartidos por los componentes del stress test.
export function fmt(n, digits = 0) {
  return Number(n).toFixed(digits);
}

export function clamp(v, min, max) {
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
}

export function uid(prefix = 'id') {
  return prefix + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);
}

export function debounce(fn, ms) {
  let t = null;
  return (...args) => {
    clearTimeout(t);
    t = setTimeout(() => fn(...args), ms);
  };
}

export function groupBy(arr, key) {
  const m = new Map();
  for (const it of arr) {
    const k = typeof key === 'function' ? key(it) : it[key];
    if (!m.has(k)) m.set(k, []);
    m.get(k).push(it);
  }
  return m;
}

export function sum(arr, sel = (x) => x) {
  return arr.reduce((a, b) => a + sel(b), 0);
}
`;

    // ───────────── styles.css compartido ─────────────

    // ───────────── Entry App.svelte ─────────────

    files['/App.svelte'] = `${SCR}\n${imports.join('\n')}\nimport './stress/styles.css';\n\n  let mounted = $state(true);\n  let remountCount = $state(0);\n\n  function remount() {\n    mounted = false;\n    setTimeout(() => { mounted = true; remountCount++; }, 50);\n  }\n${END}\n\n<div class="stress-header">\n  <h1>Prueba de rendimiento — 50 componentes</h1>\n  <p>10 micro · 20 small · 15 medium · 5 large · variación extrema</p>\n</div>\n\n<div class="stress-toolbar">\n  <div>\n    <strong>${50} componentes</strong>\n    <small class="text-muted ms-2">Remount: {remountCount}</small>\n  </div>\n  <div class="d-flex gap-2">\n    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick={remount}>\n      <i class="bi bi-arrow-clockwise"></i> Volver a montar\n    </button>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n{#if mounted}\n<div class="stress-grid">\n${renderCalls.join('\n')}\n</div>\n{/if}
`;

    return files;
  }

  const SCR_END = '</' + 'script>';  // para no cerrar el script principal

  const TEMPLATES = {
    counter: {
      '/App.svelte': `<scr` + `ipt>\n  let count = $state(0);\n${SCR_END}\n\n<div class="container py-3">\n  <h2>Mi aplicación</h2>\n  <p class="text-secondary mb-3">Usa las clases de <strong>Bootstrap 5.3</strong> directamente en tu HTML.</p>\n  <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg" onclick={() => count++}>\n    <i class="bi bi-hand-thumbs-up"></i>\n    Me han pulsado {count} {count === 1 ?     },
    todo: {
      '/App.svelte': `<scr` + `ipt>\n  let items = $state(['Aprender Svelte', 'Construir mi app']);\n  let text = $state('');\n\n  function add() {\n    if (text.trim()) {\n      items = [...items, text.trim()];\n      text = '';\n    }\n  }\n  function remove(i) {\n    items = items.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i);\n  }\n${SCR_END}\n\n<h2>Mis tareas</h2>\n<div style="display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; margin-bottom: 1rem">\n  <input bind:value={text} placeholder="Escribe una tarea" onkeydown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && add()} />\n  <button onclick={add}>Añadir</button>\n</div>\n\n{#if items.length === 0}\n  <p>No hay tareas. ¡Añade la primera!</p>\n{:else}\n  <ul>\n    {#each items as item, i}\n      <li>\n        {item}\n        <button onclick={() => remove(i)}>✕</button>\n      </li>\n    {/each}\n  </ul>\n{/if}\n`,
    },
    form: {
      '/App.svelte': `<scr` + `ipt>\n  let name = $state('');\n  let email = $state('');\n  let errors = $state({});\n  let sent = $state(false);\n\n  function validate() {\n    const e = {};\n    if (!name.trim()) e.name = 'El nombre es obligatorio';\n    if (!email.includes('@')) e.email = 'Email no válido';\n    errors = e;\n    return Object.keys(e).length === 0;\n  }\n\n  function submit() {\n    if (validate()) sent = true;\n  }\n${SCR_END}\n\n<h2>Registro</h2>\n\n{#if sent}\n  <p style="color: green">¡Gracias, {name}!     },
    fetch: {

    },
    starter: {
      '/App.svelte': `<scr` + `ipt>\n  import TodoItem from './components/TodoItem.svelte';\n  import { addItem, removeItem, countActive } from './utils.js';\n  import './styles.css';\n\n  let items = $state([\n    { id: 1, text: 'Aprender Svelte 5', done: true },\n    { id: 2, text: 'Crear mi primera app', done: false },\n    { id: 3, text: 'Exportar a HTML', done: false },\n  ]);\n  let nextId = $state(4);\n  let text = $state('');\n  let filter = $state('all');  // 'all' | 'active' | 'done'\n\n  function add() {\n    const item = addItem({ id: nextId, text, done: false });\n    items = [...items, item];\n    nextId++;\n    text = '';\n  }\n  function toggle(id) {\n    items = items.map(it => it.id === id ? { ...it, done: !it.done } : it);\n  }\n  function remove(id) {\n    items = removeItem(items, id);\n  }\n\n  const filtered = $derived(\n    filter === 'active' ? items.filter(i => !i.done)\n    : filter === 'done' ? items.filter(i => i.done)\n    : items\n  );\n  const remaining = $derived(countActive(items));\n${SCR_END}\n\n<main class="app">\n  <header class="app-header">\n    <h1>Mis tareas</h1>\n    <p class="subtitle">Plantilla starter: <code>App.svelte</code> + <code>components/</code> + <code>utils.js</code> + <code>styles.css</code></p>\n  </header>\n\n  <form class="add-form" onsubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); add(); }}>\n    <input bind:value={text} placeholder="¿Qué necesitas hacer?" />\n    <button type="submit" disabled={!text.trim()}>Añadir</button>\n  </form>\n\n  <div class="filters\">\n    <button class:active={filter === 'all'} onclick={() => filter = 'all'}>Todas ({items.length})</button>\n    <button class:active={filter === 'active'} onclick={() => filter = 'active'}>Pendientes ({remaining})</button>\n    <button class:active={filter === 'done'} onclick={() => filter = 'done'}>Hechas ({items.length - remaining})</button>\n  </div>\n\n  {#if filtered.length === 0}\n    <p class="empty\">No hay tareas {filter === 'active' ? 'pendientes' : filter === 'done' ? 'hechas' : ''}.</p>\n  {:else}\n    <ul class="todo-list">\n      {#each filtered as item (item.id)}\n        <TodoItem {item} onToggle={() => toggle(item.id)} onRemove={() => remove(item.id)} />\n      {/each}\n    </ul>\n  {/if}\n</main>\n`,
      '/components/TodoItem.svelte': `<scr` + `ipt>\n  let { item, onToggle, onRemove } = $props();\n${SCR_END}\n\n<li class="todo-item" class:done={item.done}>\n  <label>\n    <input type="checkbox" checked={item.done} onchange={onToggle} />\n    <span>{item.text}</span>\n  </label>\n  <button class="remove" onclick={onRemove} aria-label="Borrar">✕</button>\n</li>\n`,
      '/utils.js': `// Helpers puros. Importables desde cualquier archivo.\nexport function addItem(item) {\n  if (!item.text || !item.text.trim()) {\n    throw new Error('El texto de la tarea no puede estar vacío');\n  }\n  return { ...item, text: item.text.trim() };\n}\n\nexport function removeItem(items, id) {\n  return items.filter(it => it.id !== id);\n}\n\nexport function countActive(items) {\n  return items.filter(it => !it.done).length;\n}\n`,
      '/styles.css': `/* Estilos globales del proyecto starter. */\n:root {\n  --accent: #ff3e00;\n  --accent-soft: #fff5f0;\n  --bg: #ffffff;\n  --fg: #1a1a1a;\n  --fg-soft: #6b7280;\n  --border: #e5e7eb;\n}\n\nbody {\n  font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif;\n  color: var(--fg);\n  margin: 0;\n  background: var(--bg);\n}\n\n.app {\n  max-width: 540px;\n  margin: 2rem auto;\n  padding: 0 1rem;\n}\n\n.app-header h1 {\n  margin: 0 0 0.3rem;\n  font-size: 1.8rem;\n}\n.app-header .subtitle {\n  margin: 0 0 1.5rem;\n  color: var(--fg-soft);\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n}\n.app-header code {\n  background: var(--accent-soft);\n  padding: 0.1rem 0.35rem;\n  border-radius: 3px;\n  font-size: 0.85em;\n}\n\n.add-form {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1rem;\n}\n.add-form input {\n  flex: 1;\n  padding: 0.6rem 0.8rem;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  outline: none;\n}\n.add-form input:focus {\n  border-color: var(--accent);\n}\n.add-form button {\n  padding: 0.6rem 1rem;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  color: white;\n  border: none;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n}\n.add-form button:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.5;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.filters {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 0.25rem;\n  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;\n}\n.filters button {\n  padding: 0.4rem 0.8rem;\n  background: transparent;\n  border: none;\n  border-bottom: 2px solid transparent;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  color: var(--fg-soft);\n}\n.filters button.active {\n  color: var(--accent);\n  border-bottom-color: var(--accent);\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n.todo-list {\n  list-style: none;\n  padding: 0;\n  margin:
